Based on this insight, we propose STEAR, a layer-aware spatiotemporal evidence intervention framework. STEAR identifies high-risk decoding steps and selects token-conditioned visual evidence from grounding-sensitive middle layers. It uses this shared evidence for two coupled purposes: restoring missing local grounding in middle layers, and constructing temporally perturbed patch-level counterfactuals to falsify inconsistent reasoning during late-layer decoding. Consequently, STEAR mitigates both spatial and temporal hallucinations within an efficient single-encode inference framework. Experiments across representative Video-LLM backbones and challenging benchmarks demonstrate that STEAR consistently reduces hallucinations while improving faithfulness, temporal consistency, and robustness. Our results confirm that reliable video decoding relies on intervening on precise evidence at the right layer, rather than enforcing a global penalty. The code is provided in the Supplementary Material.

</details>


### [75] [Can Nano Banana 2 Replace Traditional Image Restoration Models? An Evaluation of Its Performance on Image Restoration Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03061)
*Weixiong Sun,Xiang Yin,Chao Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对Nano Banana 2在图像修复任务中的系统性评估表明，通用生成模型可作为统一修复解决方案，提示设计对性能至关重要


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估通用生成AI模型（特别是Nano Banana 2）是否能作为统一的图像修复解决方案，探索其在多样化场景和退化类型中的表现

Method: 对Nano Banana 2在多样化场景和退化类型中进行系统性评估，重点关注提示设计的影响，使用简洁提示并加入显式保真度约束，与最先进的修复模型进行对比

Result: Nano Banana 2在全参考指标上优于现有修复模型，在感知质量上保持竞争力，用户研究进一步支持这一结论；在小脸、密集人群和严重退化等挑战性场景中表现出强泛化能力

Conclusion: 通用生成模型具有作为统一图像修复解决方案的强大潜力，但提示表述敏感性需要迭代优化，可控性和鲁棒性是关键挑战

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI raise the question of whether general-purpose image editing models can serve as unified solutions for image restoration. In this work, we conduct a systematic evaluation of Nano Banana 2 for image restoration across diverse scenes and degradation types. Our results show that prompt design plays a critical role, where concise prompts with explicit fidelity constraints achieve the best trade-off between reconstruction accuracy and perceptual quality. Compared with state-of-the-art restoration models, Nano Banana 2 achieves superior performance in full-reference metrics while remaining competitive in perceptual quality, which is further supported by user studies. We also observe strong generalization in challenging scenarios, such as small faces, dense crowds, and severe degradations. However, the model remains sensitive to prompt formulation and may require iterative refinement for optimal results. Overall, our findings suggest that general-purpose generative models hold strong potential as unified image restoration solvers, while highlighting the importance of controllability and robustness. All test results are available on https://github.com/yxyuanxiao/NanoBanana2TestOnIR.

</details>


### [76] [MI-Pruner: Crossmodal Mutual Information-guided Token Pruner for Efficient MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03072)
*Jiameng Li,Aleksei Tiulpin,Matthew B. Blaschko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MI-Pruner方法，通过直接计算视觉和文本特征间的互信息来剪枝视觉token，相比基于注意力分数的方法更精确高效


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型中视觉信息相对稀疏，现有视觉剪枝方法通常基于视觉编码器或LLM解码器中的注意力分数来评估token重要性，但这种方法依赖特定机制信号，不够直接

Method: 提出MI-Pruner方法，在视觉和文本特征交互前直接计算它们之间的互信息，显式衡量特征层面的跨模态依赖关系，无需访问内部注意力图或修改架构

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法优于先前基于注意力的剪枝方法，且延迟最小

Conclusion: 通过直接计算跨模态特征互信息进行视觉剪枝的方法简单、高效且非侵入式，能更精确地衡量视觉token重要性

Abstract: For multimodal large language models (MLLMs), visual information is relatively sparse compared with text. As a result, research on visual pruning emerges for efficient inference. Current approaches typically measure token importance based on the attention scores in the visual encoder or in the LLM decoder, then select visual tokens with high attention scores while pruning others. In this paper, we pursue a different and more surgical approach. Instead of relying on mechanism-specific signals, we directly compute Mutual Information (MI) between visual and textual features themselves, prior to their interaction. This allows us to explicitly measure crossmodal dependency at the feature levels. Our MI-Pruner is simple, efficient and non-intrusive, requiring no access to internal attention maps or architectural modifications. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach outperforms previous attention-based pruning methods with minimal latency.

</details>


### [77] [Revealing Physical-World Semantic Vulnerabilities: Universal Adversarial Patches for Infrared Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03117)
*Chengyin Hu,Yuxian Dong,Yikun Guo,Xiang Chen,Junqi Wu,Jiahuan Long,Yiwei Wei,Tingsong Jiang,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UCGP是一种针对红外视觉语言模型的通用物理对抗补丁框架，通过曲线网格参数化和统一表示驱动目标来破坏跨模态语义对齐


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外视觉语言模型在低能见度环境中的多模态感知应用前景广阔，但其对抗鲁棒性尚未得到充分研究。现有对抗补丁方法主要针对RGB模型设计，不适用于红外VLM的开放语义理解和物理部署需求

Method: 提出通用曲线网格补丁框架，包括：1）曲线网格网格参数化实现连续、低频、可部署的补丁生成；2）统一表示驱动目标促进子空间偏离、拓扑破坏和隐蔽性；3）元差分进化和EOT增强的TPS变形建模以提高实际部署和域偏移下的鲁棒性

Result: UCGP能够持续破坏不同IR-VLM架构的语义理解能力，同时保持跨模型可迁移性、跨数据集泛化性、真实世界物理有效性以及对防御的鲁棒性

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了当前红外多模态系统中先前被忽视的鲁棒性漏洞，UCGP框架为红外VLM的对抗鲁棒性研究提供了新视角

Abstract: Infrared vision-language models (IR-VLMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for multimodal perception in low-visibility environments, yet their robustness to adversarial attacks remains largely unexplored. Existing adversarial patch methods are mainly designed for RGB-based models in closed-set settings and are not readily applicable to the open-ended semantic understanding and physical deployment requirements of infrared VLMs. To bridge this gap, we propose Universal Curved-Grid Patch (UCGP), a universal physical adversarial patch framework for IR-VLMs. UCGP integrates Curved-Grid Mesh (CGM) parameterization for continuous, low-frequency, and deployable patch generation with a unified representation-driven objective that promotes subspace departure, topology disruption, and stealth. To improve robustness under real-world deployment and domain shift, we further incorporate Meta Differential Evolution and EOT-augmented TPS deformation modeling. Rather than manipulating labels or prompts, UCGP directly disrupts the visual representation space, weakening cross-modal semantic alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UCGP consistently compromises semantic understanding across diverse IR-VLM architectures while maintaining cross-model transferability, cross-dataset generalization, real-world physical effectiveness, and robustness against defenses. These findings reveal a previously overlooked robustness vulnerability in current infrared multimodal systems.

</details>


### [78] [Salt: Self-Consistent Distribution Matching with Cache-Aware Training for Fast Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03118)
*Xingtong Ge,Yi Zhang,Yushi Huang,Dailan He,Xiahong Wang,Bingqi Ma,Guanglu Song,Yu Liu,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SC-DMD方法通过自一致分布匹配蒸馏和缓存分布感知训练，在极低推理预算（2-4步）下提升视频生成质量，解决轨迹一致性蒸馏的过度平滑和分布匹配蒸馏的漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型蒸馏到极低推理预算（2-4步）面临挑战：轨迹一致性蒸馏在复杂视频动态下变得保守，产生过度平滑外观和弱运动；分布匹配蒸馏能恢复锐利样本，但其局部训练信号未显式正则化去噪更新的跨时间步组合，导致组合推演易漂移。

Method: 提出自一致分布匹配蒸馏（SC-DMD），显式正则化连续去噪更新的端点一致组合。针对实时自回归视频生成，将KV缓存视为质量参数化条件，提出缓存分布感知训练：在多步推演上应用SC-DMD，并引入缓存条件特征对齐目标，引导低质量输出向高质量参考对齐。

Result: 在非自回归骨干（如Wan 2.1）和自回归实时范式（如Self Forcing）上的广泛实验表明，Salt方法持续提升低NFE视频生成质量，同时保持与多种KV缓存内存机制的兼容性。

Conclusion: SC-DMD通过显式正则化去噪更新的端点一致组合，解决了现有蒸馏方法的局限性；缓存分布感知训练进一步优化实时自回归视频生成，为极低推理预算下的高质量视频生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Distilling video generation models to extremely low inference budgets (e.g., 2--4 NFEs) is crucial for real-time deployment, yet remains challenging. Trajectory-style consistency distillation often becomes conservative under complex video dynamics, yielding an over-smoothed appearance and weak motion. Distribution matching distillation (DMD) can recover sharp, mode-seeking samples, but its local training signals do not explicitly regularize how denoising updates compose across timesteps, making composed rollouts prone to drift. To overcome this challenge, we propose Self-Consistent Distribution Matching Distillation (SC-DMD), which explicitly regularizes the endpoint-consistent composition of consecutive denoising updates. For real-time autoregressive video generation, we further treat the KV cache as a quality parameterized condition and propose Cache-Distribution-Aware training. This training scheme applies SC-DMD over multi-step rollouts and introduces a cache-conditioned feature alignment objective that steers low-quality outputs toward high-quality references. Across extensive experiments on both non-autoregressive backbones (e.g., Wan~2.1) and autoregressive real-time paradigms (e.g., Self Forcing), our method, dubbed \textbf{Salt}, consistently improves low-NFE video generation quality while remaining compatible with diverse KV-cache memory mechanisms. Source code will be released at \href{https://github.com/XingtongGe/Salt}{https://github.com/XingtongGe/Salt}.

</details>


### [79] [CAMEO: A Conditional and Quality-Aware Multi-Agent Image Editing Orchestrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03156)
*Yuhan Pu,Hao Zheng,Ziqian Mo,Hill Zhang,Tianyi Fan,Shuhong Wu,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAMEO：一个结构化多智能体框架，将条件图像编辑重新定义为质量感知、反馈驱动的过程，而非一次性生成任务，通过分解为规划、结构化提示、假设生成和自适应参考接地等协调阶段，实现迭代精炼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前条件图像编辑方法（如Seedream、Nano Banana等）大多依赖单步生成范式，缺乏明确的质量控制，可能导致过度偏离原始图像、产生结构伪影或环境不一致的修改，通常需要手动提示调整才能获得可接受结果。

Method: 提出CAMEO框架，将编辑分解为协调阶段：规划、结构化提示、假设生成和自适应参考接地（仅在任务复杂度需要时调用外部指导）。通过将评估直接嵌入编辑循环中，对中间结果进行迭代精炼，形成纠正结构和上下文不一致性的闭环过程。

Result: 在异常插入和人体姿态切换任务上评估CAMEO。在多个强大的编辑骨干和独立评估模型上，CAMEO相比多个最先进模型平均获得20%更高的胜率，展示了在条件图像编辑中改进的鲁棒性、可控性和结构可靠性。

Conclusion: CAMEO通过将条件图像编辑重新构建为质量感知、反馈驱动的过程，而非一次性生成任务，显著提升了编辑质量、结构一致性和可控性，为解决复杂编辑场景中的结构控制问题提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Conditional image editing aims to modify a source image according to textual prompts and optional reference guidance. Such editing is crucial in scenarios requiring strict structural control (i.e., anomaly insertion in driving scenes and complex human pose transformation). Despite recent advances in large-scale editing models (i.e., Seedream, Nano Banana, etc), most approaches rely on single-step generation. This paradigm often lacks explicit quality control, may introduce excessive deviation from the original image, and frequently produces structural artifacts or environment-inconsistent modifications, typically requiring manual prompt tuning to achieve acceptable results. We propose \textbf{CAMEO}, a structured multi-agent framework that reformulates conditional editing as a quality-aware, feedback-driven process rather than a one-shot generation task. CAMEO decomposes editing into coordinated stages of planning, structured prompting, hypothesis generation, and adaptive reference grounding, where external guidance is invoked only when task complexity requires it. To overcome the lack of intrinsic quality control in existing methods, evaluation is embedded directly within the editing loop. Intermediate results are iteratively refined through structured feedback, forming a closed-loop process that progressively corrects structural and contextual inconsistencies. We evaluate CAMEO on anomaly insertion and human pose switching tasks. Across multiple strong editing backbones and independent evaluation models, CAMEO consistently achieves 20\% more win rate on average compared to multiple state-of-the-art models, demonstrating improved robustness, controllability, and structural reliability in conditional image editing.

</details>


### [80] [EffiMiniVLM: A Compact Dual-Encoder Regression Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03172)
*Yin-Loon Khor,Yi-Jie Wong,Yan Chai Hum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EffiMiniVLM：一种紧凑的双编码器视觉语言回归框架，用于冷启动场景下的产品质量预测，仅使用20%的Amazon Reviews数据，在保持竞争力的同时实现4-8倍的资源效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决冷启动场景下产品质量预测问题，现有视觉语言模型通常依赖大型架构和/或大量外部数据集，导致计算成本高昂。需要开发更高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出EffiMiniVLM紧凑双编码器视觉语言回归框架，集成EfficientNet-B0图像编码器和MiniLM文本编码器，搭配轻量级回归头。引入加权Huber损失函数，利用评分计数强调更可靠的样本，提高训练样本效率。

Result: 模型仅包含27.7M参数，需要6.8 GFLOPs，使用20%的Amazon Reviews 2023数据集训练，获得CES得分0.40，在基准测试中资源成本最低。与显著更大的模型相比保持竞争力，资源效率比其他前5方法高4-8倍，且是唯一不使用外部数据集的方法。将数据扩展到40%后，模型性能超过使用更大模型和数据集的其他方法。

Conclusion: EffiMiniVLM展示了紧凑视觉语言模型在冷启动产品质量预测任务中的有效性，通过高效架构设计和加权损失函数，在有限计算资源下实现竞争性能，并展现出良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Predicting product quality from multimodal item information is critical in cold-start scenarios, where user interaction history is unavailable and predictions must rely on images and textual metadata. However, existing vision-language models typically depend on large architectures and/or extensive external datasets, resulting in high computational cost. To address this, we propose EffiMiniVLM, a compact dual-encoder vision-language regression framework that integrates an EfficientNet-B0 image encoder and a MiniLM-based text encoder with a lightweight regression head. To improve training sample efficiency, we introduce a weighted Huber loss that leverages rating counts to emphasize more reliable samples, yielding consistent performance gains. Trained using only 20% of the Amazon Reviews 2023 dataset, the proposed model contains 27.7M parameters and requires 6.8 GFLOPs, yet achieves a CES score of 0.40 with the lowest resource cost in the benchmark. Despite its small size, it remains competitive with significantly larger models, achieving comparable performance while being approximately 4x to 8x more resource-efficient than other top-5 methods and being the only approach that does not use external datasets. Further analysis shows that scaling the data to 40% alone allows our model to overtake other methods, which use larger models and datasets, highlighting strong scalability despite the model's compact design.

</details>


### [81] [ProtoFlow: Mitigating Forgetting in Class-Incremental Remote Sensing Segmentation via Low-Curvature Prototype Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03212)
*Jiekai Wu,Rong Fu,Chuangqi Li,Zijian Zhang,Guangxin Wu,Hao Zhang,Shiyin Lin,Jianyuan Ni,Yang Li,Dongxu Zhang,Amir H. Gandomi,Simon Fong,Pengbin Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProtoFlow：一种时间感知的原型动态框架，通过将类别原型建模为轨迹并学习其时间演化，解决遥感分割中的增量学习问题，减少表示漂移和遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感分割在实际部署中本质上是持续性的：新的语义类别不断出现，采集条件随季节、城市和传感器而变化。现有增量学习方法将训练步骤视为孤立更新，导致表示漂移和遗忘问题未得到充分控制。

Method: 提出ProtoFlow框架，将类别原型建模为时间轨迹，通过显式的时间向量场学习原型演化。联合实施低曲率运动和类间分离约束，在整个增量学习过程中稳定原型几何结构。

Result: 在标准的类别增量和领域增量遥感基准测试中，相比强基线方法取得一致提升，包括mIoUall指标提高1.5-2.0个百分点，同时减少了遗忘现象。

Conclusion: 显式建模时间原型演化是遥感持续分割的一种实用且可解释的策略，能够增强模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: Remote sensing segmentation in real deployment is inherently continual: new semantic categories emerge, and acquisition conditions shift across seasons, cities, and sensors. Despite recent progress, many incremental approaches still treat training steps as isolated updates, which leaves representation drift and forgetting insufficiently controlled. We present ProtoFlow, a time-aware prototype dynamics framework that models class prototypes as trajectories and learns their evolution with an explicit temporal vector field. By jointly enforcing low-curvature motion and inter-class separation, ProtoFlow stabilizes prototype geometry throughout incremental learning. Experiments on standard class- and domain-incremental remote sensing benchmarks show consistent gains over strong baselines, including up to 1.5-2.0 points improvement in mIoUall, together with reduced forgetting. These results suggest that explicitly modeling temporal prototype evolution is a practical and interpretable strategy for robust continual remote sensing segmentation.

</details>


### [82] [VOSR: A Vision-Only Generative Model for Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03225)
*Rongyuan Wu,Lingchen Sun,Zhengqiang Zhang,Xiangtao Kong,Jixin Zhao,Shihao Wang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VOSR：纯视觉生成式超分辨率框架，无需多模态预训练，在感知质量和结构保真度上媲美甚至超越基于文本到图像扩散模型的方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式超分辨率方法主要依赖预训练的大规模文本到图像扩散模型，但超分辨率本质上是低分辨率输入条件下的图像恢复任务。研究是否仅使用视觉数据训练的SR模型能够与基于T2I的方法相媲美

Method: 提出VOSR纯视觉生成框架：1) 使用预训练视觉编码器从LR输入提取语义丰富且空间基础的特征作为视觉语义指导；2) 重新审视无分类器指导，提出面向恢复的指导策略，保留弱LR锚点；3) 先训练多步VOSR模型，然后蒸馏为一步模型进行高效推理

Result: VOSR训练成本不到代表性T2I-based SR方法的十分之一，在多步和一步设置下均取得竞争性或更好的感知质量和效率，在合成和真实世界基准测试中产生更忠实结构且更少幻觉

Conclusion: 首次证明高质量生成式超分辨率无需多模态预训练即可实现，纯视觉方法在感知质量、效率和结构保真度方面具有竞争力

Abstract: Most of the recent generative image super-resolution (SR) methods rely on adapting large text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models pretrained on web-scale text-image data. While effective, this paradigm starts from a generic T2I generator, despite that SR is fundamentally a low-resolution (LR) input-conditioned image restoration task. In this work, we investigate whether an SR model trained purely on visual data can rival T2I-based ones. To this end, we propose VOSR, a Vision-Only generative framework for SR. We first extract semantically rich and spatially grounded features from the LR input using a pretrained vision encoder as visual semantic guidance. We then revisit classifier-free guidance for training generative models and show that the standard unconditional branch is ill-suited to restoration models trained from scratch. We therefore replace it with a restoration-oriented guidance strategy that preserves weak LR anchors. Built upon these designs, we first train a multi-step VOSR model from scratch and then distill it into a one-step model for efficient inference. VOSR requires less than one-tenth of the training cost of representative T2I-based SR methods, yet in both multi-step and one-step settings, it achieves competitive or even better perceptual quality and efficiency, while producing more faithful structures with fewer hallucinations on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks. Our results, for the first time, show that high-quality generative SR can be achieved without multimodal pretraining. The code and models can be found at https://github.com/cswry/VOSR.

</details>


### [83] [CoME-VL: Scaling Complementary Multi-Encoder Vision-Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03231)
*Ankan Deria,Komal Kumar,Xilin He,Imran Razzak,Hisham Cholakkal,Fahad Shahbaz Khan,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoME-VL：一种融合对比训练和自监督视觉编码器的多编码器视觉语言模型框架，通过熵引导的多层聚合和RoPE增强的交叉注意力实现互补视觉表示融合，在视觉理解和定位任务上显著优于单编码器基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型通常依赖单一对比训练的视觉编码器（如CLIP），虽然擅长跨模态对齐和检索，但自监督视觉编码器（如DINO）在密集语义理解和鲁棒性方面表现更好。研究如何融合这两种互补的视觉表示以提升视觉语言模型的综合性能。

Method: 提出CoME-VL框架，融合对比训练编码器和自监督DINO编码器：1）熵引导的多层聚合，通过正交约束投影减少冗余；2）RoPE增强的交叉注意力，对齐异构token网格并生成紧凑的融合视觉token。融合后的token可注入仅解码器LLM，对标准VLM流程改动最小。

Result: 在多样化视觉语言基准测试中，CoME-VL始终优于单编码器基线：视觉理解任务平均提升4.9%，定位任务平均提升5.4%。在RefCOCO检测任务上达到最先进性能，相比基线有大幅提升。消融研究验证了层融合、非冗余特征混合和融合容量对性能的影响。

Conclusion: 融合对比训练和自监督视觉编码器的互补表示能显著提升视觉语言模型的性能。CoME-VL框架通过有效的表示级融合机制，在视觉理解和定位任务上取得一致改进，证明了多编码器融合策略的有效性。

Abstract: Recent vision-language models (VLMs) typically rely on a single vision encoder trained with contrastive image-text objectives, such as CLIP-style pretraining. While contrastive encoders are effective for cross-modal alignment and retrieval, self-supervised visual encoders often capture richer dense semantics and exhibit stronger robustness on recognition and understanding tasks. In this work, we investigate how to scale the fusion of these complementary visual representations for vision-language modeling. We propose CoME-VL: Complementary Multi-Encoder Vision-Language, a modular fusion framework that integrates a contrastively trained vision encoder with a self-supervised DINO encoder. Our approach performs representation-level fusion by (i) entropy-guided multi-layer aggregation with orthogonality-constrained projections to reduce redundancy, and (ii) RoPE-enhanced cross-attention to align heterogeneous token grids and produce compact fused visual tokens. The fused tokens can be injected into a decoder-only LLM with minimal changes to standard VLM pipelines. Extensive experiments across diverse vision-language benchmarks demonstrate that CoME-VL consistently outperforms single-encoder baselines. In particular, we observe an average improvement of 4.9% on visual understanding tasks and 5.4% on grounding tasks. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on RefCOCO for detection while improving over the baseline by a large margin. Finally, we conduct ablation studies on layer merging, non-redundant feature mixing, and fusion capacity to evaluate how complementary contrastive and self-supervised signals affect VLM performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [A Dynamic Toolkit for Transmission Characteristics of Precision Reducers with Explicit Contact Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02387)
*Jiacheng Miao,Chao Liu,Qiliang Wang,Yunhui Guan,Weidong He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于精密减速器传动特性分析的动态工具包，通过显式接触几何建模实现更高精度和计算效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精密减速器是机器人系统的关键组件，直接影响人形机器人、四足机器人、协作机器人、工业机器人和SCARA机器人的运动精度和动态性能。现有方法在准确建模接触行为、评估齿轮刚度和预测系统振动方面存在挑战。

Method: 提出统一框架，集成先进接触理论和数值求解方法，采用模块化、可脚本化架构，支持跨不同减速器拓扑的快速重构。通过显式接触几何分析传动特性。

Result: 相比传统动力学软件，提出的工具包具有更高精度和计算效率。通过已发表基准的数值验证确认了方法的准确性。

Conclusion: 该动态工具包为精密减速器传动特性分析提供了有效的解决方案，能够准确建模接触行为、评估刚度和预测振动，支持多种机器人应用中的减速器设计优化。

Abstract: Precision reducers are critical components in robotic systems, directly affecting the motion accuracy and dynamic performance of humanoid robots, quadruped robots, collaborative robots, industrial robots, and SCARA robots. This paper presents a dynamic toolkit for analyzing the transmission characteristics of precision reducers with explicit contact geometry. A unified framework is proposed to address the challenges in modeling accurate contact behaviors, evaluating gear stiffness, and predicting system vibrations. By integrating advanced contact theories and numerical solving methods, the proposed toolkit offers higher precision and computational efficiency compared to traditional dynamics software. The toolkit is designed with a modular, scriptable architecture that supports rapid reconfiguration across diverse reducer topologies. Numerical validation against published benchmarks confirms the accuracy of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [85] [Backup-Based Safety Filters: A Comparative Review of Backup CBF, Model Predictive Shielding, and gatekeeper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02401)
*Taekyung Kim,Aswin D. Menon,Akshunn Trivedi,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过统一框架比较了三种基于备份的安全过滤器方法（Backup CBF、MPS、gatekeeper），揭示了它们的共同备份策略结构和关键算法差异，并展示了MPS是gatekeeper的特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在澄清三种基于备份的安全过滤器方法（Backup CBF、MPS、gatekeeper）之间的理论联系和差异，通过统一的比较框架使它们的共同结构和关键差异更加明确。

Method: 采用统一的安全过滤器抽象和共享符号，通过比较三种方法的过滤器非活动集（即名义策略保持不变的状态集）来分析它们。特别展示了MPS是gatekeeper的特例，并将gatekeeper与Backup CBF在隐式安全集内的非活动集内部联系起来。

Result: 建立了三种方法的统一视图，揭示了基于备份的安全过滤器保守性的一个关键来源：安全性通常通过备份操作的可行性来评估，而不是通过名义策略的持续安全执行来评估。

Conclusion: 本文作为紧凑的教程和综述，澄清了这些方法之间的理论联系和差异，为理解和比较基于备份的安全过滤器提供了统一的框架。

Abstract: This paper revisits three backup-based safety filters -- Backup Control Barrier Functions (Backup CBF), Model Predictive Shielding (MPS), and gatekeeper -- through a unified comparative framework. Using a common safety-filter abstraction and shared notation, we make explicit both their common backup-policy structure and their key algorithmic differences. We compare the three methods through their filter-inactive sets, i.e., the states where the nominal policy is left unchanged. In particular, we show that MPS is a special case of gatekeeper, and we further relate gatekeeper to the interior of the Backup CBF inactive set within the implicit safe set. This unified view also highlights a key source of conservatism in backup-based safety filters: safety is often evaluated through the feasibility of a backup maneuver, rather than through the nominal policy's continued safe execution. The paper is intended as a compact tutorial and review that clarifies the theoretical connections and differences among these methods.

</details>


### [86] [F2F-AP: Flow-to-Future Asynchronous Policy for Real-time Dynamic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02408)
*Haoyu Wei,Xiuwei Xu,Ziyang Cheng,Hang Yin,Angyuan Ma,Bingyao Yu,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出利用预测物体流合成未来观测的框架，通过流对比学习对齐预测与真实未来状态，使异步策略能够主动规划以补偿延迟，提升动态操作任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步推理在机器人操作中已取得进展，但固有延迟导致生成动作滞后于实时环境，在动态场景中尤其严重，这种时间错位严重损害策略对快速变化环境的理解和反应能力。

Method: 提出新颖框架，利用预测物体流合成未来观测，结合基于流的对比学习目标，将预测观测的视觉特征表示与真实未来状态对齐，使异步策略能够基于预期视觉上下文进行主动规划和运动。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在复杂动态操作任务中显著增强了响应性和成功率。

Conclusion: 通过预测物体流和对比学习实现未来观测合成，使异步策略能够显式补偿延迟，在涉及主动移动物体的操作任务中实现鲁棒执行。

Abstract: Asynchronous inference has emerged as a prevalent paradigm in robotic manipulation, achieving significant progress in ensuring trajectory smoothness and efficiency. However, a systemic challenge remains unresolved, as inherent latency causes generated actions to inevitably lag behind the real-time environment. This issue is particularly exacerbated in dynamic scenarios, where such temporal misalignment severely compromises the policy's ability to interpret and react to rapidly evolving surroundings. In this paper, we propose a novel framework that leverages predicted object flow to synthesize future observations, incorporating a flow-based contrastive learning objective to align the visual feature representations of predicted observations with ground-truth future states. Empowered by this anticipated visual context, our asynchronous policy gains the capacity for proactive planning and motion, enabling it to explicitly compensate for latency and robustly execute manipulation tasks involving actively moving objects. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach significantly enhances responsiveness and success rates in complex dynamic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [87] [Tune to Learn: How Controller Gains Shape Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02523)
*Antonia Bronars,Younghyo Park,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文系统研究了位置控制器增益对机器人学习管道的影响，发现最优增益选择取决于学习范式而非任务行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于任务顺应性或刚度选择控制器增益的方法在与状态条件策略结合时失效，因为有效刚度源于学习反应与控制动力学的相互作用。需要研究增益选择如何影响学习算法的可学习性。

Method: 系统研究位置控制器增益对现代机器人学习管道三个核心组件的影响：行为克隆、从零开始的强化学习、以及仿真到现实的迁移。在多个任务和机器人平台上进行广泛实验。

Result: 发现：(1) 行为克隆受益于顺应性和过阻尼增益机制；(2) 强化学习在兼容的超参数调优下可在所有增益机制中成功；(3) 仿真到现实迁移受到刚性和过阻尼增益机制的损害。

Conclusion: 最优增益选择不取决于期望的任务行为，而是取决于所采用的学习范式。增益选择应基于可学习性而非传统顺应性逻辑。

Abstract: Position controllers have become the dominant interface for executing learned manipulation policies. Yet a critical design decision remains understudied: how should we choose controller gains for policy learning? The conventional wisdom is to select gains based on desired task compliance or stiffness. However, this logic breaks down when controllers are paired with state-conditioned policies: effective stiffness emerges from the interplay between learned reactions and control dynamics, not from gains alone. We argue that gain selection should instead be guided by learnability: how amenable different gain settings are to the learning algorithm in use. In this work, we systematically investigate how position controller gains affect three core components of modern robot learning pipelines: behavior cloning, reinforcement learning from scratch, and sim-to-real transfer. Through extensive experiments across multiple tasks and robot embodiments, we find that: (1) behavior cloning benefits from compliant and overdamped gain regimes, (2) reinforcement learning can succeed across all gain regimes given compatible hyperparameter tuning, and (3) sim-to-real transfer is harmed by stiff and overdamped gain regimes. These findings reveal that optimal gain selection depends not on the desired task behavior, but on the learning paradigm employed. Project website: https://younghyopark.me/tune-to-learn

</details>


### [88] [From Impact to Insight: Dynamics-Aware Proprioceptive Terrain Sensing on Granular Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02563)
*Yifeng Zhang,Yue Wu,Jake Futterman,Jacob Meseha,Eduardo Rosales,Irie Cooper,J. Diego Caporale,Feifei Qian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种基于物理的框架，用于在高速跳跃过程中通过本体感知进行动态地形表征，发现加速度相关的附加质量效应主导了瞬态力响应，传统准静态假设会导致显著误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数地形表征方法依赖准静态假设，忽略了高速动态条件下（如冲击和快速姿态转换）产生的速度和加速度相关效应。机器人需要在自然地形中运动时准确解释这些动态接触力。

Method: 通过控制冲击速度和腿部刚度的跳跃实验，测量颗粒地形相互作用。开发了物理框架：将力分解为加速度相关的附加质量效应（与脚部下方颗粒夹带相关），结合动量观测器估计器补偿刚体惯性和重力，并引入加速度感知加权回归处理高加速度事件中增加的力方差。

Result: 实验表明，准静态假设在高速跳跃中会导致颗粒地形属性估计的显著差异，特别是在触地和控制器诱导的刚度转换期间。速度相关阻力无法完全解释这些差异，加速度相关的附加质量效应主导瞬态力响应。所提方法能够在不同运动条件下一致恢复颗粒刚度参数，与线性执行器地面真值高度匹配。

Conclusion: 高速运动中的准确地形推断需要显式处理加速度相关的颗粒效应。该研究为机器人在动态探索陆地及行星环境时表征复杂可变形地形提供了基础框架。

Abstract: Robots that traverse natural terrain must interpret contact forces generated under highly dynamic conditions. However, most terrain characterization approaches rely on quasi-static assumptions that neglect velocity- and acceleration-dependent effects arising during impact and rapid stance transitions. In this work, we investigate granular terrain interaction during high-speed hopping and develop a physics-based framework for dynamic terrain characterization using proprioceptive sensing alone. Through controlled hopping experiments with systematically varied impact speed and leg compliance, our measurements reveal that quasi-static based assumptions lead to large discrepancies in granular terrain property estimation during high-speed hopping, particularly upon touchdown and controller-induced stiffness transitions. Velocity-dependent drag alone cannot explain these discrepancies. Instead, acceleration-dependent added-mass effects-associated with grain entrainment beneath the foot-dominate transient force responses. We integrate this force decomposition with a momentum-observer-based estimator that compensates for rigid-body inertia and gravity, and introduce an acceleration-aware weighted regression to account for increased force variance during high-acceleration events. Together, these methods enable consistent recovery of granular stiffness parameters across locomotion conditions, closely matching linear-actuator ground truth. Our results demonstrate that accurate terrain inference during high-speed locomotion requires explicit treatment of acceleration-dependent granular effects, and provide a foundation for robots to characterize complex deformable terrain during dynamic exploration of terrestrial and planetary environments.

</details>


### [89] [Differentiable SpaTiaL: Symbolic Learning and Reasoning with Geometric Temporal Logic for Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02643)
*Licheng Luo,Kaier Liang,Cristian-Ioan Vasile,Mingyu Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Differentiable SpaTiaL，首个端到端可微符号时空逻辑工具箱，通过构建平滑的几何原语实现从高层语义规范到底层几何配置的可微映射


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空逻辑在梯度优化中存在局限性：可微时序逻辑忽略物体-环境交互关系，而考虑交互的空间逻辑方法依赖离散几何引擎导致计算图断裂，无法进行精确梯度传播

Method: 开发完全张量化的工具箱，直接在多边形集合上构建平滑、自动微分兼容的几何原语，解析推导关键空间谓词（有符号距离、相交、包含、方向关系）的可微松弛

Result: 实现了两个核心能力：(1) 在严格时空约束下进行大规模并行轨迹优化，(2) 通过反向传播直接从演示中学习空间逻辑参数，实验验证了框架的有效性和可扩展性

Conclusion: Differentiable SpaTiaL克服了现有方法的局限性，为复杂操作任务提供了端到端可微的符号时空逻辑框架，支持约束优化和参数学习

Abstract: Executing complex manipulation in cluttered environments requires satisfying coupled geometric and temporal constraints. Although Spatio-Temporal Logic (SpaTiaL) offers a principled specification framework, its use in gradient-based optimization is limited by non-differentiable geometric operations. Existing differentiable temporal logics focus on the robot's internal state and neglect interactive object-environment relations, while spatial logic approaches that capture such interactions rely on discrete geometry engines that break the computational graph and preclude exact gradient propagation. To overcome this limitation, we propose Differentiable SpaTiaL, a fully tensorized toolbox that constructs smooth, autograd-compatible geometric primitives directly over polygonal sets. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first end-to-end differentiable symbolic spatio-temporal logic toolbox. By analytically deriving differentiable relaxations of key spatial predicates--including signed distance, intersection, containment, and directional relations--we enable an end-to-end differentiable mapping from high-level semantic specifications to low-level geometric configurations, without invoking external discrete solvers. This fully differentiable formulation unlocks two core capabilities: (i) massively parallel trajectory optimization under rigorous spatio-temporal constraints, and (ii) direct learning of spatial logic parameters from demonstrations via backpropagation. Experimental results validate the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed framework.Code Available: https://github.com/plen1lune/DiffSpaTiaL

</details>


### [90] [A Rapid Instrument Exchange System for Humanoid Robots in Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02707)
*Bingcong Zhang,Yihang Lyv,Lianbo Ma,Yushi He,Pengfei Wei,Xingchi Liu,Jinhua Li,Jianchang Zhao,Lizhi Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于单轴柔性对接和环境约束释放的沉浸式遥操作快速器械交换系统，用于人形机器人手术中的器械切换


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人用于微创手术时，其固有的双臂配置需要高效的器械交换能力来执行复杂手术，模拟外科医生手动切换器械的自然工作流程

Method: 采用基于单轴柔性对接和环境约束释放的低延迟机制，结合头戴式显示器提供的实时第一人称视角感知，构建沉浸式遥操作系统

Result: 专家与新手对比评估显示系统具有高操作鲁棒性和快速收敛的学习曲线；新手经过简短训练后器械装卸性能显著提升；验证了人形机器人在受限临床环境中执行稳定器械交换的技术可行性

Conclusion: 系统显著降低了对接过程中的操作复杂性和认知负荷，虽然长距离空间对齐在时间成本和协作稳定性方面仍存在挑战，但成功验证了人形机器人执行稳定器械交换的技术可行性

Abstract: Humanoid robot technologies have demonstrated immense potential for minimally invasive surgery (MIS). Unlike dedicated multi-arm surgical platforms, the inherent dual-arm configuration of humanoid robots necessitates an efficient instrument exchange capability to perform complex procedures, mimicking the natural workflow where surgeons manually switch instruments. To address this, this paper proposes an immersive teleoperated rapid instrument exchange system. The system utilizes a low-latency mechanism based on single-axis compliant docking and environmental constraint release. Integrated with real-time first-person view (FPV) perception via a head-mounted display (HMD), this framework significantly reduces operational complexity and cognitive load during the docking process. Comparative evaluations between experts and novices demonstrate high operational robustness and a rapidly converging learning curve; novice performance in instrument attachment and detachment improved substantially after brief training. While long-distance spatial alignment still presents challenges in time cost and collaborative stability, this study successfully validates the technical feasibility of humanoid robots executing stable instrument exchanges within constrained clinical environments.

</details>


### [91] [V2X-QA: A Comprehensive Reasoning Dataset and Benchmark for Multimodal Large Language Models in Autonomous Driving Across Ego, Infrastructure, and Cooperative Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02710)
*Junwei You,Pei Li,Zhuoyu Jiang,Weizhe Tang,Zilin Huang,Rui Gan,Jiaxi Liu,Yan Zhao,Sikai Chen,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: V2X-QA是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型在自动驾驶中多视角推理能力的真实世界数据集和基准测试，涵盖车辆端、基础设施端和协同视角，通过专家验证的多选题框架进行细粒度诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶基准测试主要关注自我中心视角，无法系统评估模型在基础设施中心和协同驾驶条件下的性能。需要开发能够评估多视角推理能力的基准测试，以推动自动驾驶中多视角协同推理的研究。

Method: 构建V2X-QA真实世界数据集，采用视图解耦评估协议，在统一的多选题问答框架下支持车辆端、基础设施端和协同驾驶条件的受控比较。基准测试按感知、预测、推理和规划三个领域组织成12个任务，通过专家验证的标注实现细粒度诊断。同时提出V2X-MoE基准对齐基线模型，采用显式视图路由和视角特定的LoRA专家。

Result: 对10个代表性SOTA专有和开源模型的基准测试结果显示：视角可访问性显著影响性能；基础设施端推理支持有意义的宏观交通理解；协同推理仍然具有挑战性，需要跨视图对齐和证据整合而非简单增加视觉输入。V2X-MoE表现出色，表明显式视角专业化是多视图推理的有前景方向。

Conclusion: V2X-QA为研究连接自动驾驶中的多视角推理、可靠性和协同物理智能提供了基础。数据集和V2X-MoE资源已公开，推动了自动驾驶中多视角协同推理的研究进展。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential for autonomous driving, yet existing benchmarks remain largely ego-centric and therefore cannot systematically assess model performance in infrastructure-centric and cooperative driving conditions. In this work, we introduce V2X-QA, a real-world dataset and benchmark for evaluating MLLMs across vehicle-side, infrastructure-side, and cooperative viewpoints. V2X-QA is built around a view-decoupled evaluation protocol that enables controlled comparison under vehicle-only, infrastructure-only, and cooperative driving conditions within a unified multiple-choice question answering (MCQA) framework. The benchmark is organized into a twelve-task taxonomy spanning perception, prediction, and reasoning and planning, and is constructed through expert-verified MCQA annotation to enable fine-grained diagnosis of viewpoint-dependent capabilities. Benchmark results across ten representative state-of-the-art proprietary and open-source models show that viewpoint accessibility substantially affects performance, and infrastructure-side reasoning supports meaningful macroscopic traffic understanding. Results also indicate that cooperative reasoning remains challenging since it requires cross-view alignment and evidence integration rather than simply additional visual input. To address these challenges, we introduce V2X-MoE, a benchmark-aligned baseline with explicit view routing and viewpoint-specific LoRA experts. The strong performance of V2X-MoE further suggests that explicit viewpoint specialization is a promising direction for multi-view reasoning in autonomous driving. Overall, V2X-QA provides a foundation for studying multi-perspective reasoning, reliability, and cooperative physical intelligence in connected autonomous driving. The dataset and V2X-MoE resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/junwei0001/V2X-QA.

</details>


### [92] [Learning Locomotion on Complex Terrain for Quadrupedal Robots with Foot Position Maps and Stability Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02744)
*Matthew Hwang,Yubin Liu,Ryo Hakoda,Takeshi Oishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合足部位置映射和动态稳定性奖励的注意力框架，用于四足机器人在复杂地形上的精确稳定运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法依赖关节角度隐式推断足部位置，缺乏优化方法的显式精度和稳定性保证，需要改进复杂地形上的四足运动

Method: 引入高度图中的足部位置映射和动态运动稳定性奖励，结合注意力框架实现复杂地形上的精确稳定运动

Result: 方法在训练域内和域外地形上都实现了精确稳定运动，提高了运动成功率

Conclusion: 提出的足部位置映射和动态稳定性奖励方法有效提升了四足机器人在复杂地形上的运动精度和稳定性

Abstract: Quadrupedal locomotion over complex terrain has been a long-standing research topic in robotics. While recent reinforcement learning-based locomotion methods improve generalizability and foot-placement precision, they rely on implicit inference of foot positions from joint angles, lacking the explicit precision and stability guarantees of optimization-based approaches. To address this, we introduce a foot position map integrated into the heightmap, and a dynamic locomotion-stability reward within an attention-based framework to achieve locomotion on complex terrain. We validate our method extensively on terrains seen during training as well as out-of-domain (OOD) terrains. Our results demonstrate that the proposed method enables precise and stable movement, resulting in improved locomotion success rates on both in-domain and OOD terrains.

</details>


### [93] [Geometrically-Constrained Radar-Inertial Odometry via Continuous Point-Pose Uncertainty Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02745)
*Wooseong Yang,Dongjae Lee,Minwoo Jung,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种几何约束的雷达-惯性里程计与建图方法，通过联合整合点和位姿不确定性，利用连续轨迹模型估计任意时间戳的位姿不确定性，结合异方差测量不确定性实现动态观测置信度评估，构建高保真地图提升里程计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达里程计在恶劣环境中对鲁棒定位至关重要，但可靠回波的稀疏性和独特的噪声特性限制了其性能。现有方法在处理雷达点云稀疏性和噪声方面存在不足，需要更有效的不确定性建模和几何约束整合方法。

Method: 采用连续轨迹模型估计任意时间戳的位姿不确定性，通过控制点传播不确定性；将位姿不确定性连续整合到异方差测量不确定性中，实现动态观测置信度评估和自适应降权无信息雷达点；利用量化的不确定性构建高保真地图；在雷达-惯性里程计中引入显式几何约束。

Result: 在多样化真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在准确性和效率方面均优于现有基线方法，实现了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 提出的几何约束雷达-惯性里程计与建图框架通过联合整合点和位姿不确定性，有效解决了雷达数据稀疏性和噪声问题，显式几何约束与不确定性感知建图框架的结合显著提升了系统性能。

Abstract: Radar odometry is crucial for robust localization in challenging environments; however, the sparsity of reliable returns and distinctive noise characteristics impede its performance. This paper introduces geometrically-constrained radar-inertial odometry and mapping that jointly consolidates point and pose uncertainty. We employ the continuous trajectory model to estimate the pose uncertainty at any arbitrary timestamp by propagating uncertainties of the control points. These pose uncertainties are continuously integrated with heteroscedastic measurement uncertainty during point projection, thereby enabling dynamic evaluation of observation confidence and adaptive down-weighting of uninformative radar points. By leveraging quantified uncertainties in radar mapping, we construct a high-fidelity map that improves odometry accuracy under imprecise radar measurements. Moreover, we reveal the effectiveness of explicit geometrical constraints in radar-inertial odometry when incorporated with the proposed uncertainty-aware mapping framework. Extensive experiments on diverse real-world datasets demonstrate the superiority of our method, yielding substantial performance improvements in both accuracy and efficiency compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [94] [Vision-Based End-to-End Learning for UAV Traversal of Irregular Gaps via Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02779)
*Linzuo Zhang,Yu Hu,Feng Yu,Yang Deng,Wenxian Yu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种完全基于视觉的端到端框架，使无人机能够直接根据深度图像生成控制指令，穿越复杂且未见过的狭窄不规则缝隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖显式缝隙提取和测量，而现有端到端方法通常假设规则形状缝隙，导致泛化能力差和实用性有限。需要一种能够处理复杂不规则缝隙的自主导航方案。

Method: 采用完全基于视觉的端到端框架，将深度图像直接映射到控制指令。在SE(3)空间中工作，结合可微分模拟、Stop-Gradient算子和双模态初始化分布实现稳定穿越。引入两个辅助预测模块：缝隙穿越成功分类器和可穿越性预测器。

Result: 广泛的仿真和真实世界实验证明了该方法的有效性、泛化能力和实际鲁棒性，能够成功穿越连续复杂缝隙。

Conclusion: 该框架为无人机在检查、搜救和灾难响应等应用中穿越狭窄不规则缝隙提供了实用且泛化能力强的解决方案，克服了传统方法和现有端到端方法的局限性。

Abstract: -Navigation through narrow and irregular gaps is an essential skill in autonomous drones for applications such as inspection, search-and-rescue, and disaster response. However, traditional planning and control methods rely on explicit gap extraction and measurement, while recent end-to-end approaches often assume regularly shaped gaps, leading to poor generalization and limited practicality. In this work, we present a fully vision-based, end-to-end framework that maps depth images directly to control commands, enabling drones to traverse complex gaps within unseen environments. Operating in the Special Euclidean group SE(3), where position and orientation are tightly coupled, the framework leverages differentiable simulation, a Stop-Gradient operator, and a Bimodal Initialization Distribution to achieve stable traversal through consecutive gaps. Two auxiliary prediction modules-a gap-crossing success classifier and a traversability predictor-further enhance continuous navigation and safety. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate the approach's effectiveness, generalization capability, and practical robustness.

</details>


### [95] [Learning Structured Robot Policies from Vision-Language Models via Synthetic Neuro-Symbolic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02812)
*Alessandro Adami,Tommaso Tubaldo,Marco Todescato,Ruggero Carli,Pietro Falco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种神经符号方法，使用视觉语言模型从多模态输入生成可执行的行为树策略，通过合成数据集训练，实现了从仿真到真实机器人的成功迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视觉语言模型的端到端机器人策略缺乏可解释性和模块化，难以应用于安全关键场景。传统机器人系统使用结构化策略表示具有可解释性、模块化和反应式执行优势。研究旨在探索如何将基础模型专门化，生成基于多模态感知的结构化机器人策略，弥合高维学习和符号控制之间的差距。

Method: 提出神经符号方法：使用视觉语言模型从视觉观察、自然语言指令和结构化系统规范合成可执行的行为树策略。引入自动化流水线生成合成多模态数据集，包含域随机化场景和基础模型生成的指令-策略对，无需人工标注即可实现可扩展监督。

Result: 在两个机器人操作臂上的真实世界实验表明，完全从合成监督学习的结构化策略能够成功迁移到物理系统。基础模型能够生成可解释的结构化机器人策略，为多模态机器人决策提供了不透明端到端方法的替代方案。

Conclusion: 基础模型可以适应生成可解释和结构化的机器人策略，为多模态机器人决策提供了一种替代不透明端到端方法的新途径。该方法结合了学习方法的灵活性和符号方法的可解释性，在安全关键应用中具有潜力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have recently demonstrated strong capabilities in mapping multimodal observations to robot behaviors. However, most current approaches rely on end-to-end visuomotor policies that remain opaque and difficult to analyze, limiting their use in safety-critical robotic applications. In contrast, classical robotic systems often rely on structured policy representations that provide interpretability, modularity, and reactive execution. This work investigates how foundation models can be specialized to generate structured robot policies grounded in multimodal perception, bridging high-dimensional learning and symbolic control. We propose a neuro-symbolic approach in which a VLM synthesizes executable Behavior Tree policies from visual observations, natural language instructions, and structured system specifications. To enable scalable supervision without manual annotation, we introduce an automated pipeline that generates a synthetic multimodal dataset of domain-randomized scenes paired with instruction-policy examples produced by a foundation model. Real-world experiments on two robotic manipulators show that structured policies learned entirely from synthetic supervision transfer successfully to physical systems. The results indicate that foundation models can be adapted to produce interpretable and structured robot policies, providing an alternative to opaque end-to-end approaches for multimodal robot decision making.

</details>


### [96] [MFE: A Multimodal Hand Exoskeleton with Interactive Force, Pressure and Thermo-haptic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02820)
*Ziyuan Tang,Yitian Guo,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多模态反馈外骨骼(MFE)，通过力、压力和热反馈增强机器人遥操作中的触觉感知


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉设备通常提供单模态反馈，无法传递丰富的多模态感官反馈（力、压力、热感觉），限制了虚拟现实和机器人遥操作中的情境感知能力

Method: 设计多模态反馈外骨骼(MFE)：1) 20自由度手部姿态捕捉；2) 主动力反馈机制（3.5-8.1N推拉力）；3) 基于电渗原理的指尖平面执行器（2.47kPa接触压力）；4) 热电热泵（10-55°C温度渲染）；集成到X-Arm 6和Inspire Hand机器人遥操作系统

Result: 用户研究显示参与者成功识别和操作可变形物体，区分不同温度的远程物体，证明MFE增强了情境感知、可用性和遥操作系统的透明度

Conclusion: MFE通过提供力、压力和热反馈的多模态触觉反馈，有效增强了机器人遥操作系统的情境感知能力和用户体验

Abstract: Recent advancements in virtual reality and robotic teleoperation have greatly increased the variety of haptic information that must be conveyed to users. While existing haptic devices typically provide unimodal feedback to enhance situational awareness, a gap remains in their ability to deliver rich, multimodal sensory feedback encompassing force, pressure, and thermal sensations. To address this limitation, we present the Multimodal Feedback Exoskeleton (MFE), a hand exoskeleton designed to deliver hybrid haptic feedback. The MFE features 20 degrees of freedom for capturing hand pose. For force feedback, it employs an active mechanism capable of generating 3.5-8.1 N of pushing and pulling forces at the fingers' resting pose, enabling realistic interaction with deformable objects. The fingertips are equipped with flat actuators based on the electro-osmotic principle, providing pressure and vibration stimuli and achieving up to 2.47 kPa of contact pressure to render tactile sensations. For thermal feedback, the MFE integrates thermoelectric heat pumps capable of rendering temperatures from 10 to 55 degrees Celsius. We validated the MFE by integrating it into a robotic teleoperation system using the X-Arm 6 and Inspire Hand manipulator. In user studies, participants successfully recognized and manipulated deformable objects and differentiated remote objects with varying temperatures. These results demonstrate that the MFE enhances situational awareness, as well as the usability and transparency of robotic teleoperation systems.

</details>


### [97] [Orientation Matters: Learning Radiation Patterns of Multi-Rotor UAVs In-Flight to Enhance Communication Availability Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02827)
*Martin Zoula,Daniel Bonilla Licea,Jan Faigl,Václav Navrátil,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过校准飞行数据学习异构四旋翼无人机对天线辐射模式的方法，使用球谐函数或加权平均建模，通过多项式系数线性回归同时解耦两个无人机的辐射模式，在真实数据集上达到3.6 dB RMS误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实应用中，无人机设置经常变化（如有效载荷改变），需要快速重新校准天线辐射模式，以实现精确的自主路径规划和集群控制。传统方法难以同时解耦异构无人机对的辐射模式。

Method: 使用球谐函数级数或诱导样本加权平均建模辐射模式；通过多项式系数线性回归同时解耦两个独立无人机的辐射模式；设计联合校准轨迹，在无障碍消声高度利用可用飞行时间。

Result: 在真实数据集上评估，成功学习两个辐射模式，达到3.6 dB RMS误差（与测量噪声水平相当），验证了方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 提出的辐射模式学习和解耦方法可用于有效载荷变化时的快速重新校准，从而在设置变化的实际应用中实现精确的自主路径规划和集群控制。

Abstract: The paper presents an approach for learning antenna Radiation Patterns (RPs) of a pair of heterogeneous quadrotor Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) by calibration flight data. RPs are modeled either as a Spherical Harmonics series or as a weighted average over inducing samples. Linear regression of polynomial coefficients simultaneously decouples the two independent UAVs' RPs. A joint calibration trajectory exploits available flight time in an obstacle-free anechoic altitude. Evaluation on a real-world dataset demonstrates the feasibility of learning both radiation patterns, achieving 3.6 dB RMS error, the measurement noise level. The proposed RP learning and decoupling can be exploited in rapid recalibration upon payload changes, thereby enabling precise autonomous path planning and swarm control in real-world applications where setup changes are expected.

</details>


### [98] [RAGE: A Tightly Coupled Radar-Aided Grip Estimator For Autonomous Race Cars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02892)
*Davide Malvezzi,Nicola Musiu,Eugenio Mascaro,Francesco Iacovacci,Marko Bertogna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAGE：一种仅使用标准传感器（IMU和雷达）实时估计车辆速度、轮胎侧偏角和侧向力的新型估计器，适用于自动驾驶赛车在极限工况下的安全操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶赛车需要在物理极限下安全高效运行，实时估计车辆-轮胎-路面摩擦至关重要。传统方法依赖昂贵、专用的传感器，需要定制安装，限制了可扩展性和部署。

Method: 提出RAGE（实时估计器），仅使用标准传感器（如IMU和雷达）同时推断车辆速度、轮胎侧偏角和作用于轮胎的侧向力。该方法适用于大多数现代自动驾驶平台。

Result: 通过高保真仿真和EAV-24自动驾驶赛车的真实世界实验验证了该方法，证明了在估计车辆侧向动力学方面的准确性和有效性。

Conclusion: RAGE提供了一种使用标准传感器实时估计车辆-轮胎-路面摩擦的可行方案，解决了传统方法依赖昂贵专用传感器的问题，为自动驾驶赛车在极限工况下的安全操作提供了支持。

Abstract: Real-time estimation of vehicle-tire-road friction is critical for allowing autonomous race cars to safely and effectively operate at their physical limits. Traditional approaches to measure tire grip often depend on costly, specialized sensors that require custom installation, limiting scalability and deployment. In this work, we introduce RAGE, a novel real-time estimator that simultaneously infers the vehicle velocity, slip angles of the tires and the lateral forces that act on them, using only standard sensors, such as IMUs and RADARs, which are commonly available on most of modern autonomous platforms. We validate our approach through both high-fidelity simulations and real-world experiments conducted on the EAV-24 autonomous race car, demonstrating the accuracy and effectiveness of our method in estimating the vehicle lateral dynamics.

</details>


### [99] [Learning Task-Invariant Properties via Dreamer: Enabling Efficient Policy Transfer for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02911)
*Junyang Liang,Yuxuan Liu,Yabin Chang,Junfan Lin,Junkai Ji,Hui Li,Changxin Huang,Jianqiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamTIP框架在Dreamer世界模型中融入任务不变特性学习，通过LLM识别接触稳定性等地形不变特性作为辅助预测目标，结合混合回放缓冲和正则化约束，显著提升四足机器人在复杂地形上的仿真到现实迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决四足机器人在多样动态地形上运动时仿真与现实条件差异带来的挑战，传统仿真到现实迁移方法依赖人工特征设计或昂贵的现实微调，需要更高效鲁棒的迁移方案。

Method: 提出DreamTIP框架：1) 利用大语言模型识别任务不变特性（如接触稳定性、地形间隙）；2) 将这些特性作为辅助预测目标整合到Dreamer世界模型中；3) 设计混合回放缓冲和正则化约束的高效适应策略，防止表示崩溃和灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在Stair、Climb、Tilt、Crawl等复杂地形实验中显著优于现有基线：1) 在8个仿真迁移任务中平均性能提升28.1%；2) 现实Climb任务中，基线成功率仅10%，而DreamTIP达到100%成功率。

Conclusion: 将任务不变特性融入Dreamer学习为机器人运动控制提供了新颖的鲁棒可迁移解决方案，有效克服仿真与现实差异，在复杂动态地形上实现高性能四足机器人运动。

Abstract: Achieving quadruped robot locomotion across diverse and dynamic terrains presents significant challenges, primarily due to the discrepancies between simulation environments and real-world conditions. Traditional sim-to-real transfer methods often rely on manual feature design or costly real-world fine-tuning. To address these limitations, this paper proposes the DreamTIP framework, which incorporates Task-Invariant Properties learning within the Dreamer world model architecture to enhance sim-to-real transfer capabilities. Guided by large language models, DreamTIP identifies and leverages Task-Invariant Properties, such as contact stability and terrain clearance, which exhibit robustness to dynamic variations and strong transferability across tasks. These properties are integrated into the world model as auxiliary prediction targets, enabling the policy to learn representations that are insensitive to underlying dynamic changes. Furthermore, an efficient adaptation strategy is designed, employing a mixed replay buffer and regularization constraints to rapidly calibrate to real-world dynamics while effectively mitigating representation collapse and catastrophic forgetting. Extensive experiments on complex terrains, including Stair, Climb, Tilt, and Crawl, demonstrate that DreamTIP significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both simulated and real-world environments. Our method achieves an average performance improvement of 28.1% across eight distinct simulated transfer tasks. In the real-world Climb task, the baseline method achieved only a 10\ success rate, whereas our method attained a 100% success rate. These results indicate that incorporating Task-Invariant Properties into Dreamer learning offers a novel solution for achieving robust and transferable robot locomotion.

</details>


### [100] [Open-Loop Planning, Closed-Loop Verification: Speculative Verification for VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02965)
*Zihua Wang,Zhitao Lin,Ruibo Li,Yu Zhang,Xu Yang,Siya Mi,Xiu-Shen Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SV-VLA框架结合了高效的开环长时程规划与轻量级闭环在线验证，通过低频宏规划器生成动作块和规划上下文，轻量级验证器持续监控执行，在动态环境中实现高效可靠的VLA控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在操作任务中表现出色但推理成本高。虽然动作分块方法能提高效率，但开环执行对环境变化敏感且缺乏闭环反馈容易导致误差累积。需要一种既能保持效率又能提供闭环鲁棒性的解决方案。

Method: 提出SV-VLA框架：1) 使用重型VLA作为低频宏规划器生成动作块和规划上下文；2) 轻量级验证器基于最新观察持续监控执行；3) 验证器结合当前观察和规划上下文，比较规划动作与闭环参考动作，仅在必要时触发重新规划。

Result: 实验证明SV-VLA结合了分块预测的效率和闭环控制的鲁棒性，在动态环境中实现了高效可靠的VLA控制。代码已开源。

Conclusion: SV-VLA框架成功解决了VLA控制中效率与鲁棒性的平衡问题，通过宏规划与轻量验证的结合，为动态环境中的具身控制提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, as large foundation models for embodied control, have shown strong performance in manipulation tasks. However, their performance comes at high inference cost. To improve efficiency, recent methods adopt action chunking, which predicts a sequence of future actions for open-loop execution. Although effective for reducing computation, open-loop execution is sensitive to environmental changes and prone to error accumulation due to the lack of close-loop feedback. To address this limitation, we propose Speculative Verification for VLA Control (SV-VLA), a framework that combines efficient open-loop long-horizon planning with lightweight closed-loop online verification. Specifically, SV-VLA uses a heavy VLA as a low-frequency macro-planner to generate an action chunk together with a planning context, while a lightweight verifier continuously monitors execution based on the latest observations. Conditioned on both the current observation and the planning context, the verifier compares the planned action against a closed-loop reference action and triggers replanning only when necessary. Experiments demonstrate that SV-VLA combines the efficiency of chunked prediction with the robustness of closed-loop control, enabling efficient and reliable VLA-based control in dynamic environments. Code is available: https://github.com/edsad122/SV-VLA.

</details>


### [101] [A Flow Matching Framework for Soft-Robot Inverse Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03006)
*Hang Yang,Fangju Yang,Yangming Zhang,Ibrahim Alsarraj,Yuhao Wang,Zhenye Luo,Zixi Chen,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于流匹配的软体连续机器人逆动力学学习框架，将逆动力学重构为条件流匹配问题，采用Rectified Flow生成物理一致的控制输入，相比传统回归方法将轨迹跟踪RMSE降低50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体连续机器人逆动力学学习面临高维非线性和复杂驱动耦合的挑战。传统反馈控制器存在控制抖振问题，确定性回归学习方法难以捕捉复杂的非线性映射以实现精确动态跟踪。

Method: 提出开环前馈控制的逆动力学框架，将逆动力学重构为条件流匹配问题，采用Rectified Flow作为轻量级实例生成物理一致的控制输入而非条件平均值。引入两个变体：RF-Physical利用物理先验进行残差建模；RF-FWD在流匹配中集成前向动力学一致性损失。

Result: 框架将轨迹跟踪RMSE降低超过50%（相比MLP、LSTM、Transformer等基线）。系统在1.14 m/s的峰值末端执行器速度下保持稳定开环执行，推理延迟低于1毫秒（0.995 ms）。

Conclusion: 流匹配是学习软体机器人系统微分逆动力学的鲁棒高性能范式，能够生成物理一致的控制输入，显著提升轨迹跟踪精度和系统性能。

Abstract: Learning the inverse dynamics of soft continuum robots remains challenging due to high-dimensional nonlinearities and complex actuation coupling. Conventional feedback-based controllers often suffer from control chattering due to corrective oscillations, while deterministic regression-based learners struggle to capture the complex nonlinear mappings required for accurate dynamic tracking. Motivated by these limitations, we propose an inverse-dynamics framework for open-loop feedforward control that learns the system's differential dynamics as a generative transport map. Specifically, inverse dynamics is reformulated as a conditional flow-matching problem, and Rectified Flow (RF) is adopted as a lightweight instance to generate physically consistent control inputs rather than conditional averages. Two variants are introduced to further enhance physical consistency: RF-Physical, utilizing a physics-based prior for residual modeling; and RF-FWD, integrating a forward-dynamics consistency loss during flow matching. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our framework reduces trajectory tracking RMSE by over 50% compared to standard regression baselines (MLP, LSTM, Transformer). The system sustains stable open-loop execution at a peak end-effector velocity of 1.14 m/s with sub-millisecond inference latency (0.995 ms). This work demonstrates flow matching as a robust, high-performance paradigm for learning differential inverse dynamics in soft robotic systems.

</details>


### [102] [Asymptotically-Bounded 3D Frontier Exploration enhanced with Bayesian Information Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03008)
*John Lewis,Meysam Basiri,Pedro U. Lima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于OctoMap的边界探索算法，具有可预测的渐近有界性能，复杂度为O(|F|)，通过前向和逆向传感器建模实现高效边界检测，并集成贝叶斯回归器估计信息增益，显著提升探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模环境中的机器人探索计算成本高昂，传统方法复杂度随环境规模增长，需要开发具有可预测性能边界的高效探索算法。

Method: 采用基于OctoMap的边界探索框架，通过战略性的前向和逆向传感器建模实现近似但高效的边界检测与维护，复杂度控制在O(|F|)。集成贝叶斯回归器估计信息增益，避免显式统计未知体素。

Result: 仿真显示该方法比现有OctoMap方法计算效率更高，与不依赖OctoMap的基线方法相当。贝叶斯增强框架相比标准确定性边界基线在不同空间尺度下总探索时间提升达54%，同时保证任务完成。

Conclusion: 提出的算法实现了可预测的渐近有界性能，复杂度与边界数量线性相关，通过贝叶斯增强显著提升探索效率，实际实验验证了计算边界和增强效果的有效性。

Abstract: Robotic exploration in large-scale environments is computationally demanding due to the high overhead of processing extensive frontiers. This article presents an OctoMap-based frontier exploration algorithm with predictable, asymptotically bounded performance. Unlike conventional methods whose complexity scales with environment size, our approach maintains a complexity of $\mathcal{O}(|\mathcal{F}|)$, where $|\mathcal{F}|$ is the number of frontiers. This is achieved through strategic forward and inverse sensor modeling, which enables approximate yet efficient frontier detection and maintenance. To further enhance performance, we integrate a Bayesian regressor to estimate information gain, circumventing the need to explicitly count unknown voxels when prioritizing viewpoints. Simulations show the proposed method is more computationally efficient than the existing OctoMap-based methods and achieves computational efficiency comparable to baselines that are independent of OctoMap. Specifically, the Bayesian-enhanced framework achieves up to a $54\%$ improvement in total exploration time compared to standard deterministic frontier-based baselines across varying spatial scales, while guaranteeing task completion. Real-world experiments confirm the computational bounds as well as the effectiveness of the proposed enhancement.

</details>


### [103] [Behavior-Constrained Reinforcement Learning with Receding-Horizon Credit Assignment for High-Performance Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03023)
*Siwei Ju,Jan Tauberschmidt,Oleg Arenz,Peter van Vliet,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出行为约束强化学习框架，通过预测机制和参考轨迹条件化，在保持专家行为一致性的同时超越演示性能，在赛车仿真中验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在局限性：强化学习能找到高性能策略但偏离人类行为，模仿学习受限于演示质量且难以超越专家数据。需要一种既能超越演示性能又能保持专家行为一致性的方法。

Method: 提出行为约束强化学习框架，包含两个关键创新：1）引入滑动时域预测机制，建模短期未来轨迹并提供前瞻奖励；2）策略以参考轨迹为条件，表示专家一致行为的分布而非单一确定性目标。

Result: 在高保真赛车仿真中使用专业车手数据评估，学习到的策略在保持与专家驾驶行为紧密对齐的同时获得有竞争力的圈速，在性能和模仿质量上均优于基线方法。人类在环仿真显示策略能再现专业车手的设置依赖驾驶特性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够学习既最优又行为一致的高性能控制策略，可作为复杂控制系统中人类决策的可靠替代，在极端动态和狭窄性能裕度的领域具有应用价值。

Abstract: Learning high-performance control policies that remain consistent with expert behavior is a fundamental challenge in robotics. Reinforcement learning can discover high-performing strategies but often departs from desirable human behavior, whereas imitation learning is limited by demonstration quality and struggles to improve beyond expert data. We propose a behavior-constrained reinforcement learning framework that improves beyond demonstrations while explicitly controlling deviation from expert behavior. Because expert-consistent behavior in dynamic control is inherently trajectory-level, we introduce a receding-horizon predictive mechanism that models short-term future trajectories and provides look-ahead rewards during training. To account for the natural variability of human behavior under disturbances and changing conditions, we further condition the policy on reference trajectories, allowing it to represent a distribution of expert-consistent behaviors rather than a single deterministic target. Empirically, we evaluate the approach in high-fidelity race car simulation using data from professional drivers, a domain characterized by extreme dynamics and narrow performance margins. The learned policies achieve competitive lap times while maintaining close alignment with expert driving behavior, outperforming baseline methods in both performance and imitation quality. Beyond standard benchmarks, we conduct human-grounded evaluation in a driver-in-the-loop simulator and show that the learned policies reproduce setup-dependent driving characteristics consistent with the feedback of top-class professional race drivers. These results demonstrate that our method enables learning high-performance control policies that are both optimal and behavior-consistent, and can serve as reliable surrogates for human decision-making in complex control systems.

</details>


### [104] [ARM: Advantage Reward Modeling for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03037)
*Yiming Mao,Zixi Yu,Weixin Mao,Yinhao Li,Qirui Hu,Zihan Lan,Minzhao Zhu,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ARM框架通过相对优势估计替代稀疏奖励，使用三态标注策略减少人工标注成本，在长时程机器人操作任务中实现99.4%成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作中，稀疏奖励难以指导信用分配，而密集进度奖励成本高且不适用于非单调行为（如回溯和恢复）

Method: 提出优势奖励建模（ARM）框架，使用三态标注策略（进步、退步、停滞）训练相对优势估计器，实现自动化进度标注，并集成到离线RL流程中进行自适应动作-奖励重加权

Result: 在挑战性长时程毛巾折叠任务中达到99.4%成功率，相比当前VLA基线在稳定性和数据效率方面均有提升，策略训练期间近乎零人工干预

Conclusion: ARM通过相对优势估计有效解决了长时程操作中的奖励稀疏问题，三态标注策略显著降低了人工标注成本，为实际机器人应用提供了高效解决方案

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation remains challenging for reinforcement learning (RL) because sparse rewards provide limited guidance for credit assignment. Practical policy improvement thus relies on richer intermediate supervision, such as dense progress rewards, which are costly to obtain and ill-suited to non-monotonic behaviors such as backtracking and recovery. To address this, we propose Advantage Reward Modeling (ARM), a framework that shifts from hard-to-quantify absolute progress to estimating relative advantage. We introduce a cost-effective tri-state labeling strategy -- Progressive, Regressive, and Stagnant -- that reduces human cognitive overhead while ensuring high cross-annotator consistency. By training on these intuitive signals, ARM enables automated progress annotation for both complete demonstrations and fragmented DAgger-style data. Integrating ARM into an offline RL pipeline allows for adaptive action-reward reweighting, effectively filtering suboptimal samples. Our approach achieves a 99.4% success rate on a challenging long-horizon towel-folding task, demonstrating improved stability and data efficiency over current VLA baselines with near-zero human intervention during policy training.

</details>


### [105] [Enhancing Multi-Robot Exploration Using Probabilistic Frontier Prioritization with Dirichlet Process Gaussian Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03042)
*John Lewis Devassy,Meysam Basiri,Mário A. T. Figueiredo,Pedro U. Lima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于概率前沿优先级的增强方法，通过Dirichlet过程高斯混合模型和概率信息增益公式，提升多智能体自主探索算法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体自主探索在环境监测、搜救和工业规模监视等应用中至关重要，但在通信约束下的有效协调仍然是一个重大挑战。现有前沿探索算法在确定最大化探索收益的最佳下一个视点时存在改进空间。

Method: 提出一种对现有前沿探索算法的增强方法，引入概率前沿优先级机制。该方法利用Dirichlet过程高斯混合模型（DP-GMM）和概率信息增益公式，改进前沿优先级质量。将这一增强集成到两种最先进的多智能体探索算法中。

Result: 该方法在不同杂乱程度、通信约束和团队规模的环境中均能持续提升性能。仿真显示两种算法在所有组合下平均分别获得10%和14%的增益。在双无人机系统的真实世界实验中成功部署进一步验证了这些发现。

Conclusion: 提出的概率前沿优先级增强方法能有效提升多智能体自主探索算法的性能，在仿真和真实世界实验中均表现出显著改进。

Abstract: Multi-agent autonomous exploration is essential for applications such as environmental monitoring, search and rescue, and industrial-scale surveillance. However, effective coordination under communication constraints remains a significant challenge. Frontier exploration algorithms analyze the boundary between the known and unknown regions to determine the next-best view that maximizes exploratory gain. This article proposes an enhancement to existing frontier-based exploration algorithms by introducing a probabilistic approach to frontier prioritization. By leveraging Dirichlet process Gaussian mixture model (DP-GMM) and a probabilistic formulation of information gain, the method improves the quality of frontier prioritization. The proposed enhancement, integrated into two state-of-the-art multi-agent exploration algorithms, consistently improves performance across environments of varying clutter, communication constraints, and team sizes. Simulations showcase an average gain of $10\%$ and $14\%$ for the two algorithms across all combinations. Successful deployment in real-world experiments with a dual-drone system further corroborates these findings.

</details>


### [106] [Joint Prediction of Human Motions and Actions in Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03065)
*Alessandra Bulanti,Alessandro Carfì,Fulvio Mastrogiovanni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MA-HERP：一个用于联合估计和预测人类动作与运动的层次递归概率框架，支持人机协作中的实时推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流畅的人机协作需要机器人持续估计人类行为并预测未来意图，这需要同时推理连续运动和离散动作，而现有方法通常将这两者孤立建模

Method: 提出MA-HERP框架，包含：(1)层次表示：通过Allen区间关系将运动组合成动作；(2)统一概率因子分解：耦合连续动态、离散标签和持续时间；(3)递归推理方案：受贝叶斯滤波启发，交替进行自上而下的动作预测和自下而上的感官证据整合

Result: 基于肌肉骨骼模拟的神经模型初步实验显示：准确的运动预测、噪声下的鲁棒动作推断，以及与人机在线协作兼容的计算性能

Conclusion: MA-HERP框架能够有效联合估计和预测人类动作与运动，为人机协作中的实时推理提供了可行的解决方案

Abstract: Fluent human--robot collaboration requires robots to continuously estimate human behaviour and anticipate future intentions. This entails reasoning jointly about \emph{continuous movements} and \emph{discrete actions}, which are still largely modelled in isolation. In this paper, we introduce \textsf{MA-HERP}, a hierarchical and recursive probabilistic framework for the \emph{joint estimation and prediction} of human movements and actions. The model combines: (i) a hierarchical representation in which movements compose into actions through admissible Allen interval relations, (ii) a unified probabilistic factorisation coupling continuous dynamics, discrete labels, and durations, and (iii) a recursive inference scheme inspired by Bayesian filtering, alternating top-down action prediction with bottom-up sensory evidence. We present a preliminary experimental evaluation based on neural models trained on musculoskeletal simulations of reaching movements, showing accurate motion prediction, robust action inference under noise, and computational performance compatible with on-line human--robot collaboration.

</details>


### [107] [Flash-Mono: Feed-Forward Accelerated Gaussian Splatting Monocular SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03092)
*Zicheng Zhang,Ke Wu,Xiangting Meng,Keyu Liu,Jieru Zhao,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Flash-Mono是一个基于前馈预测的单目3D高斯溅射SLAM系统，通过多帧上下文直接预测高斯属性，避免了逐帧优化，实现了10倍加速，同时通过2D高斯面元和隐藏状态闭环模块提升了几何精度和多视角一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单目3D高斯溅射SLAM存在时间效率低、几何精度差和多视角一致性不足的问题，这些问题源于"从零开始训练"的优化过程耗时且单帧几何先验缺乏帧间尺度一致性。作者认为需要一种前馈范式，利用多帧上下文直接预测高斯属性来解决这些挑战。

Method: 系统包含三个核心模块：1) 前馈预测前端：训练循环前馈模型，通过交叉注意力逐步聚合多帧视觉特征到隐藏状态，联合预测相机位姿和逐像素高斯属性；2) 2D高斯溅射映射后端：用2D高斯面元替代传统3D高斯椭球体提升几何保真度；3) 高效隐藏状态闭环模块：利用隐藏状态作为紧凑子图描述符，实现高效闭环和全局Sim(3)优化来消除漂移。

Result: Flash-Mono实现了10倍加速，同时确保了高质量渲染。在跟踪和建图质量方面达到最先进性能，有效解决了漂移问题，提升了几何精度和多视角一致性。

Conclusion: Flash-Mono通过前馈预测范式成功解决了传统单目3D高斯溅射SLAM的效率、精度和一致性挑战，展示了在具身感知和实时重建应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Monocular 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM suffers from critical limitations in time efficiency, geometric accuracy, and multi-view consistency. These issues stem from the time-consuming $\textit{Train-from-Scratch}$ optimization and the lack of inter-frame scale consistency from single-frame geometry priors. We contend that a feed-forward paradigm, leveraging multi-frame context to predict Gaussian attributes directly, is crucial for addressing these challenges. We present Flash-Mono, a system composed of three core modules: a feed-forward prediction frontend, a 2D Gaussian Splatting mapping backend, and an efficient hidden-state-based loop closure module. We trained a recurrent feed-forward frontend model that progressively aggregates multi-frame visual features into a hidden state via cross attention and jointly predicts camera poses and per-pixel Gaussian properties. By directly predicting Gaussian attributes, our method bypasses the burdensome per-frame optimization required in optimization-based GS-SLAM, achieving a $\textbf{10x}$ speedup while ensuring high-quality rendering. The power of our recurrent architecture extends beyond efficient prediction. The hidden states act as compact submap descriptors, facilitating efficient loop closure and global $\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ optimization to mitigate the long-standing challenge of drift. For enhanced geometric fidelity, we replace conventional 3D Gaussian ellipsoids with 2D Gaussian surfels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Flash-Mono achieves state-of-the-art performance in both tracking and mapping quality, highlighting its potential for embodied perception and real-time reconstruction applications. Project page: https://victkk.github.io/flash-mono.

</details>


### [108] [An Open-Source LiDAR and Monocular Off-Road Autonomous Navigation Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03096)
*Rémi Marsal,Quentin Picard,Adrien Poiré,Sébastien Kerbourc'h,Thibault Toralba,Clément Yver,Alexandre Chapoutot,David Filliat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个开源越野导航系统，支持LiDAR和单目3D感知，无需任务特定训练，通过Depth Anything V2和VINS-Mono实现单目深度估计，在仿真和真实环境中性能接近高分辨率LiDAR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野自主导航需要可靠的3D感知，但LiDAR成本高、功耗大，而基于基础模型的单目深度估计作为轻量级替代方案在户外导航中的应用尚未充分探索。

Method: 结合零样本深度预测（Depth Anything V2）和稀疏SLAM测量（VINS-Mono）进行度量深度重标定，采用边缘掩码减少障碍物幻觉和时间平滑缓解SLAM不稳定性，生成点云后构建机器人中心2.5D高程地图用于基于代价地图的规划。

Result: 在逼真仿真（Isaac Sim）和真实非结构化环境中评估，单目配置在大多数场景中匹配高分辨率LiDAR性能，证明基于基础模型的单目深度估计是越野导航中可行的LiDAR替代方案。

Conclusion: 开源导航栈和仿真环境提供了完整的越野导航流程和可复现基准，基于基础模型的单目深度估计能够实现稳健的越野导航，是LiDAR的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Off-road autonomous navigation demands reliable 3D perception for robust obstacle detection in challenging unstructured terrain. While LiDAR is accurate, it is costly and power-intensive. Monocular depth estimation using foundation models offers a lightweight alternative, but its integration into outdoor navigation stacks remains underexplored. We present an open-source off-road navigation stack supporting both LiDAR and monocular 3D perception without task-specific training. For the monocular setup, we combine zero-shot depth prediction (Depth Anything V2) with metric depth rescaling using sparse SLAM measurements (VINS-Mono). Two key enhancements improve robustness: edge-masking to reduce obstacle hallucination and temporal smoothing to mitigate the impact of SLAM instability. The resulting point cloud is used to generate a robot-centric 2.5D elevation map for costmap-based planning. Evaluated in photorealistic simulations (Isaac Sim) and real-world unstructured environments, the monocular configuration matches high-resolution LiDAR performance in most scenarios, demonstrating that foundation-model-based monocular depth estimation is a viable LiDAR alternative for robust off-road navigation. By open-sourcing the navigation stack and the simulation environment, we provide a complete pipeline for off-road navigation as well as a reproducible benchmark. Code available at https://github.com/LARIAD/Offroad-Nav.

</details>


### [109] [FSUNav: A Cerebrum-Cerebellum Architecture for Fast, Safe, and Universal Zero-Shot Goal-Oriented Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03139)
*Mingao Tan,Yiyang Li,Shanze Wang,Xinming Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FSUNav提出了一种脑-小脑架构，用于快速、安全、通用的零样本目标导航，通过整合视觉语言模型和深度强化学习，实现跨异构机器人平台的高效安全导航


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言导航方法存在异构机器人兼容性差、实时性能不足、导航安全性低等问题，且难以支持开放词汇语义泛化和多模态任务输入

Method: 提出FSUNav架构：小脑模块采用基于深度强化学习的通用局部规划器；大脑模块构建三层推理模型，利用视觉语言模型建立端到端检测验证机制，支持多模态输入

Result: 在MP3D、HM3D和OVON基准测试中达到最先进性能，在物体、实例图像和任务导航上显著优于现有方法；在多种机器人平台上的实际部署验证了其鲁棒性和实用性

Conclusion: FSUNav通过创新的脑-小脑架构成功解决了视觉语言导航中的关键瓶颈，实现了快速、安全、通用的零样本目标导航，具有优异的泛化能力和实际应用价值

Abstract: Current vision-language navigation methods face substantial bottlenecks regarding heterogeneous robot compatibility, real-time performance, and navigation safety. Furthermore, they struggle to support open-vocabulary semantic generalization and multimodal task inputs. To address these challenges, this paper proposes FSUNav: a Cerebrum-Cerebellum architecture for fast, safe, and universal zero-shot goal-oriented navigation, which innovatively integrates vision-language models (VLMs) with the proposed architecture. The cerebellum module, a high-frequency end-to-end module, develops a universal local planner based on deep reinforcement learning, enabling unified navigation across heterogeneous platforms (e.g., humanoid, quadruped, wheeled robots) to improve navigation efficiency while significantly reducing collision risk. The cerebrum module constructs a three-layer reasoning model and leverages VLMs to build an end-to-end detection and verification mechanism, enabling zero-shot open-vocabulary goal navigation without predefined IDs and improving task success rates in both simulation and real-world environments. Additionally, the framework supports multimodal inputs (e.g., text, target descriptions, and images), further enhancing generalization, real-time performance, safety, and robustness. Experimental results on MP3D, HM3D, and OVON benchmarks demonstrate that FSUNav achieves state-of-the-art performance on object, instance image, and task navigation, significantly outperforming existing methods. Real-world deployments on diverse robotic platforms further validate its robustness and practical applicability.

</details>


### [110] [The Compression Gap: Why Discrete Tokenization Limits Vision-Language-Action Model Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03191)
*Takuya Shiba*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现，在视觉-语言-动作模型中，当动作表示为离散标记时，升级视觉编码器无法提升下游操作性能，这与视觉-语言模型中的预期相反。原因在于"压缩间隙"原理：视觉运动管道中的扩展行为受最紧信息瓶颈位置控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解为什么在视觉-语言-动作模型中，升级视觉编码器（这在视觉-语言建模中能提升性能）在动作表示为离散标记时却无法改善下游操作性能。研究者希望揭示这种性能差异背后的根本机制。

Method: 通过信息论原理"压缩间隙"来解释现象，并在LIBERO基准上进行三方面验证：1) 因子实验比较不同模型规模下Diffusion Policy和OAT的性能变化；2) 使用四个编码器测试编码器质量梯度；3) 代码本容量实验，通过放松代码本容量来验证瓶颈假设。

Result: 实验结果显示：Diffusion Policy通过升级编码器性能提升超过21个百分点，而OAT的增益显著减弱；Diffusion Policy的性能随编码器质量单调提升，而OAT保持平坦；放松代码本容量能部分恢复编码器敏感性，为瓶颈假设提供了因果证据。

Conclusion: 在物理AI中实现有效扩展需要识别管道中的信息瓶颈位置，而不是简单地统一增加模型或数据规模。当动作通过固定容量代码本离散化时，代码本成为绑定约束，上游编码器的改进无法通过该瓶颈传播。

Abstract: Scaling Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models by upgrading the vision encoder is expected to improve downstream manipulation performance--as it does in vision-language modeling. We show that this expectation fails when actions are represented as discrete tokens, and explain why through an information-theoretic principle we call the Compression Gap: in any visuomotor pipeline, scaling behavior is governed by the location of the tightest information bottleneck. When actions are continuous (e.g., Diffusion Policy), the vision encoder is the binding constraint, and upgrading it directly improves performance. When actions are discretized through a fixed-capacity codebook (e.g., OAT), the codebook becomes the binding constraint, and encoder improvements cannot propagate past it--regardless of how rich the upstream representation is. We validate this principle on the LIBERO benchmark with three lines of evidence: a factorial experiment showing that encoder upgrades improve Diffusion Policy by over 21 percentage points while OAT gains are substantially attenuated across model scales; an encoder quality gradient across four encoders confirming that Diffusion Policy tracks encoder quality monotonically while OAT remains flat; and a codebook size experiment demonstrating that relaxing codebook capacity partially recovers encoder sensitivity, providing causal evidence for the bottleneck hypothesis. Our findings reveal that scaling in Physical AI requires identifying where information bottlenecks lie in the pipeline, rather than uniformly increasing model or data size.

</details>


### [111] [Safety-Critical Centralized Nonlinear MPC for Cooperative Payload Transportation by Two Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03200)
*Ruturaj S. Sambhus,Yicheng Zeng,Kapi Ketan Mehta,Jeeseop Kim,Kaveh Akbari Hamed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种安全关键的中心化非线性模型预测控制框架，用于两个四足机器人的协作负载运输，通过控制屏障函数确保避障安全，并在真实硬件实验中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决两个四足机器人协作运输负载时的安全控制问题，特别是在复杂环境中需要同时确保机器人和负载的避障安全，同时处理系统动力学耦合、质量惯性不确定性和外部扰动等挑战。

Method: 采用基于控制屏障函数(CBF)的非线性模型预测控制(NMPC)框架，将机器人-负载系统建模为离散时间非线性微分代数系统，保留交互力作为决策变量，形成结构化DAE约束最优控制问题，实现实时高效求解。

Result: 在Unitree Go2平台上进行了大量硬件实验，验证了算法在复杂环境中的有效性，能够处理质量惯性不确定性和外部推力扰动，成功实现安全协作负载运输。

Conclusion: 提出的CBF-NMPC框架能够有效解决多四足机器人协作负载运输的安全控制问题，在真实硬件环境中表现出鲁棒性和实时性，为复杂环境下的多机器人协作提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a safety-critical centralized nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) framework for cooperative payload transportation by two quadrupedal robots. The interconnected robot-payload system is modeled as a discrete-time nonlinear differential-algebraic system, capturing the coupled dynamics through holonomic constraints and interaction wrenches. To ensure safety in complex environments, we develop a control barrier function (CBF)-based NMPC formulation that enforces collision avoidance constraints for both the robots and the payload. The proposed approach retains the interaction wrenches as decision variables, resulting in a structured DAE-constrained optimal control problem that enables efficient real-time implementation. The effectiveness of the algorithm is validated through extensive hardware experiments on two Unitree Go2 platforms performing cooperative payload transportation in cluttered environments under mass and inertia uncertainty and external push disturbances.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Xpertbench: Expert Level Tasks with Rubrics-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02368)
